# All closed Apple pull requests: exhaustive historical audit

## Scope, evidence, and limits

This audit covers **all 919 closed PRs** returned by GitHub for `kiwix/kiwix-apple`, including the repository's earlier `kiwix/apple` naming: **850 merged and 69 closed-unmerged**. A closed-unmerged proposal is **abandoned/unmerged evidence**, not a shipped fix. Creation dates span 2017-04-05 through 2026-10-28. The snapshot was retrieved on 2026-10-29; the exact UTC timestamps are in the raw artifact. PR #1804 is included. Open PRs are outside this requested closed-PR scope.

Authoritative evidence is [apple-all-closed-prs.raw.json.xz](apple-all-closed-prs.raw.json.xz). It retains original complete GitHub PR-list objects (including every body and merge timestamp), source diffs, complete repository issue-comment and inline-review-comment pages, linked-issue bodies, and machine-readable per-number classifications. Secondary evidence-only model summaries are identified as analysis, never substituted for the actual patch.

Pagination completed with 10 pages of **100,100,100,100,100,100,100,100,100,19** PRs and an explicitly fetched **empty page 11**. Repository issue comments completed at **51 pages / 5,018 items**; inline review comments at **5 pages / 416 items**. Of those, **1,694 discussion comments** belong to the 919 audited closed PRs. No title/date cutoff or recent/sample selection was used for the inventory. All PR bodies and all associated issue-comment, inline-review-comment and submitted-review bodies were read/analyzed in full. Detailed relevant diffs were analyzed for **630 PRs** (328 focused full diffs, including the actual #1590 workflow, and 302 additional historical implementation diffs); **289** remaining non-application/configuration/resource PRs received complete body/discussion/file-inventory classifications. Submitted reviews were additionally completed for **all 919 PRs: 623 reviews, including 192 nonblank bodies**. A REST rate limit affected 309 review endpoints; 8 GraphQL batches recovered all of them, each with `hasNextPage=false`, rather than silently accepting a partial API result.

Merged patches are read-only `git diff <merge-sha>^1 <merge-sha>` from `../kiwix-apple` when available; missing objects and unmerged branch diffs use GitHub's PR diff representation. These are patch evidence, not current checked-out source or proof of runtime behavior. GitHub rejected oversized unmerged #89's diff (>300 files); the alternative paginated files endpoint retained **352 file records/patches over 4 pages**. The HTTP 406 and successful alternative are both preserved. GitHub-provided binary/oversized-file patch omissions remain omissions, not invented source review.

The Apple reference working tree and current Tauri implementation were compared by source inspection only. Tauri was being actively integrated during this audit, so file/line anchors describe the inspected snapshot rather than immutable commits. In particular, search cancellation changed from missing to source-covered during inspection. **No build, test suite, formatter, simulator, hardware action, or runtime check was executed by this audit. No row means “all fixed.”** Historical author's test reports remain attributed historical claims, not independent acceptance. Only these two named audit files were written; application/shared files and reference repositories were unchanged. Current checked-out Apple source corroborates its block policy (`WebContentBlocker.swift:32–50`), 1 GB warning (`ZimFileDetail.swift:344–374`), actual OPDS exception handling (`OPDSParser.mm:56–67`), explicit session command (`App_macOS.swift:107–113`), and still-disabled download Settings insertion (`App_macOS.swift:154–156`).

## Highest-risk actionable acceptance

Status: **SC** = corresponding current source contract located, not runtime verified; **PARTIAL** = only part of the historical observable behavior is present; **MISSING** = precise current path lacks the behavior; **NOT-BASELINE** = unmerged, reverted, inactive, branded-only, or infrastructure cannot be promoted to an implemented standard-app feature. Every acceptance below still needs the owning main agent's platform execution where applicable.

| Priority / historical PRs | Actual Apple contract and current implementation comparison | Observable acceptance / remaining path |

Apple's actual #1743 patch permits `zim:`, `blob:`, `data:` after #1700 blanked MapLibre maps; do not port only the restrictive earlier patch. | Open a real MapLibre/routing map: tiles and blob workers run; external image/script/fetch/WebSocket requests, including `https://host/pixel?u=zim://`, do not reach the network. Check startup and preloaded tabs, not just a settled page. |

Current `server.rs:833` uses `frame-src 'none'`; `permissions.rs:3–6` explicitly relies on no nested frames for origin-safe camera consent. | A same-archive iframe must render without enabling an external iframe or allowing a nested frame to acquire a sensor. ## Non-features, abandoned proposals, and misleading chronology

* **Live Activities are not a retained standard-app baseline.** #1096 added them; #1130 disabled them; #1134 restored them; #1144–#1146 explored background updates; unmerged #1147 records OS suspension limitations. #1172 disabled the integration again, #1181 removed the plist remainder and #1186 removed residual framework/scheduling integration. A merged earlier addition does not override the later removal. Do not claim lock-screen progress or direct pause/resume is implemented today from these PRs.
* **Arbitrary macOS download location:** unmerged #1494 and #1541 describe direct-to-external-volume streaming; they are not implemented Apple features. Merged #1546 is disabled settings groundwork (commented-out Settings insertion, no changed download transport). Its comments defer activation/downloader integration to open follow-up #1549. Tauri's own direct-to-destination/resumable acquisition can be an improvement without inventing Apple baseline behavior.
* **Geolocation:** unmerged #1554 bridge proposal and #1561 entitlement-only alternative were superseded by merged #1562. Count one implemented bridge contract, then track #1582 target-specific entitlement scope. A permission string alone does not deliver location.
* **Cookies and cloud:** unmerged #1666 was replaced by merged #1679; #1681 adds a limited CookieStore wrapper. #1680 stores `Documents/zimCookies.json`; neither its title nor a local Documents file proves iCloud cross-device synchronization.
* **Range requests:** #496's early implementation was rolled back by #502. #768, #805 and #897 were closed-unmerged; #894 is the later merged implementation, dependent on #896's task-ownership fix. Earlier failure proposals are not extra implemented capabilities.
* **Native session restore:** #1603 implements an explicit Restore Previous Session command, not automatic replay of every window at startup. Historical changes #968/#1030 fixed external opening while earlier restoration was broken. Compare actual grouping and key-window semantics, not just the word “restore.”
* **Spelling:** #1326 is feature-flagged and default-off for the standard app, initially intended for DWDS; #1362 changes the flag name. #1454 limits a libzim cache to 16 MiB, **not all application memory**. Unmerged #1433/#1434 disable/revert proposals are not adopted fixes.
* **Release/App Store/CI/signing work:** build runners, XcodeGen, certificates, merchant IDs, notarization, TestFlight upload, App Store assets, version/build counters and privacy-manifest packaging are separately classified below. They can be required to distribute an app, but do not demonstrate working readers, hardware grants, background tasks, or successful App Store approval. #546's historical secret-bearing `pull_request_target` workflow is followed by #1590 removing that trigger; neither becomes an archive-reader feature.
* **Reverts and partial rewrites remain visible:** #315→#316→#317, #402→#403, #459→#460→#461 and many old broad version-named PRs contain real code and are not dismissed as release-number bumps. Dormant map/Quick Look/branded paths are distinguished from enabled features in the per-number notes.

## Current implementation reference map

These are consolidation points, **not blanket resolution claims**. Each per-PR row links to its group; where no individual Tauri acceptance is established, the row remains scoped historical evidence and inherits only the explicit acceptance above.

| Group | Current Tauri comparison surface | Applicability |
|---|---|---|
| reader-security | `src-tauri/src/server.rs`, `permissions.rs`; `src/components/Reader.tsx`; `src/reader-bridge.js` | Relevant all platforms; isolation must survive native sensor integrations. |
| reader-media | `server.rs:489–554`; `archive.rs`; Reader iframe lifecycle; native webview OS behavior | Relevant; HTTP bytes are source-covered, fullscreen/inline/PiP still needs platform execution. |
| search | `src-tauri/src/search.rs`, `archive.rs`, `spelling.rs`; Reader/UnifiedSearch | Relevant; cancellation/source bounds do not prove every ranking/UI edge. |
| catalog | `src-tauri/src/catalog.rs`; `src/components/Catalog.tsx`; archive filter components | Relevant; cache/304 differs from Apple's persisted full catalog. |
| acquisition | `src-tauri/src/acquisition.rs`, `publication.rs`, `mobile_service.rs`; Downloads | Relevant; 1 GB warning and native background execution are separate acceptance. |
| persistence | `store.rs`, `storage.rs`, `windows.rs`, `migration.rs`; `reader-storage.ts`, `storage-bridge.js`, App | Relevant core state; Apple's opaque profile/database formats are not automatically migrated. |
| import-storage | `imports.rs`, `library_files.rs`, `mobile_rebase.rs`; native DocumentStorage/plugin | Relevant; external-provider/volume revocation needs native execution. |
| sharing | `sharing.rs`; SharingControls; mobile lifecycle/native plugin | Relevant; explicit opt-in and honest network lifecycle; no cloud billing or emulator changed here. |
| accessibility | Reader/Library/Catalog named buttons, landmarks, focus and live regions | Relevant; source hooks are not a screen-reader run. |
| localization | `src/i18n.tsx`, `src/locales/`; `src-tauri/src/localization.rs`; native labels | Relevant; completeness is locale-by-locale, not proof from a localization PR title. |
| platform-ui | App/Reader/styles, `windows.rs`, `menus.rs`, mobile plugin/HardwareShortcuts | Relevant observable navigation/keyboard/geometry behavior, not mandatory replication of every obsolete native view layout. |
| diagnostics | `diagnostics.rs`; `service.rs` integrity command; Library detail UI | Relevant; persisted per-book integrity and diagnostic run/report integration missing in inspected snapshot. |
| branded-only | Standard reader/shared code only when behavior is not gated out | Optional product packaging/custom-brand policy is not a missing standard Kiwix feature. Shared crash/security fixes still relevant. |
| release-infrastructure | Tauri/Xcode/CI/distribution configuration, not the reader API | Platform delivery requirements; upstream Apple credentials/merchant settings are not implemented application functionality. |
| release-metadata / documentation / maintenance | Current version/build/docs/licenses/dependencies; affected shared module if any | Non-feature unless actual diff establishes an observable behavioral contract. Historical dependency update does not prove current SDK safety. |

## Exhaustive per-PR ledger

Every closed PR number appears exactly once below. **M** means GitHub `merged_at` is non-null; **U** means closed-unmerged (abandoned/unmerged, never counted as a fix). Dates are merge dates for M, close dates for U. `D` means complete relevant source-diff/discussion analysis; `I` means body + complete discussion + changed-file inventory classification for non-feature/configuration/resource scope. Generated/binary metadata and patches omitted by GitHub are not claimed as implementation review. Category maps to the current comparison surface above; historical notes are not current runtime acceptance. Full unabridged behavior/caveats and links remain in raw `classifications`.

| PR / state / date | Classification / review tier | Actual change and caveats |
|---|---|---|
| [#59 — Handle iTunes activity metadata in git #58](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/59) **M** 2017-04-14 | release-metadata / I | Tracks iTunes/App Store description and changelog text in git; no runtime feature evidenced. Caveat: File lists only. Comments request English edits and relocation/renaming to AppStore/WhatsNew in #61; that rename is not established here. |
| [#61 — Corrected a couple of typos and tried to improve the description.](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/61) **M** 2017-04-17 | release-metadata / I | Edits App Store description wording and changelog metadata. Caveat: Comment reports description updated for version 1.8.1 pending review, not publication. Listed changelog remains changelog.md despite the WhatsNew naming plan discussed in #59. |
| [#66 — macOS 2.0](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/66) **M** 2017-08-30 | platform-ui / D | Replaces the macOS placeholder web page with a local ZIM reader: Open selects one file and replaces loaded books, saves read-only security-scoped bookmarks for reopening at launch, loads the main page, and provides Home/Back/Forward actions. Invalid files hide the reader and show an alert. A search popup displays up to 20 full-text results with snippets, falling back to title suggestions only when full-text returns none; selecting a result opens its article. Local content is served through kiwix:// URLs. Closing the last window terminates the app. Also changes the shared catalog endpoint to library_zim.xml. Caveat: Merged, but no build or runtime verification is supplied. The shared legacy search execution and result aggregation are commented out, so this is not evidence of working iOS search; the shared bridging header also still imports the deleted ZimReader.h. macOS title suggestions divide by reader count without guarding zero loaded books, and cancellation can bypass search cleanup. The preferences controller only sets a window title; no functional preferences are demonstrated. Project/storyboard inventories alone do not establish wiring. |
| [#67 — new readme](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/67) **M** 2017-08-30 | documentation / I | README update only; no runtime functionality evidenced. Caveat: No text diff supplied, so documentation details are unknown. |
| [#68 — Kiwix for macOS 2.0 (2.0.3)](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/68) **M** 2017-08-31 | import-storage / D | Adds a macOS application openFiles handler that passes supplied paths to the main window's book-opening workflow. That workflow replaces loaded books, persists read-only security-scoped bookmarks, clears search, and loads a main page or shows the invalid-ZIM alert. The Open dialog now restricts selection to .zim and .zimaa files and remains single-selection; the application callback accepts multiple paths. Caveat: Merged, without supplied runtime verification. The openFiles handler returns without opening anything if the main-window controller is unavailable; no deferred handling is shown. File associations and entitlements appear only in the changed-file inventory, so Finder registration and sandbox behavior are not independently established by the supplied diff. |
| [#88 — buttons and bars](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/88) **M** 2018-01-10 | platform-ui / D | Replaces the main tab-thumbnail collection controller with a search-and-panel UI. Toolbar configuration places back, forward, contents, bookmark, library and settings icons in navigation bars at regular width and a bottom toolbar at compact width. Adds animated panel show/hide and dim-overlay dismissal; search cancellation deactivates search, and selecting a result dismisses search and hides a compact-width panel. Also deletes legacy operations, help HTML, tab/home controllers and utility code. Caveat: Incomplete UI refactor despite merge: the new toolbar dispatcher performs no actions, custom UIButton views have no touch targets wired in the supplied code, and library presentation, article loading, contents retrieval and container integration remain commented out. Search-result selection and library 'Open Main Page' do not load content. Toolbar setup is invoked on size-class/size changes, not explicitly in viewDidLoad. Deleted search and CloudKit code already contained unfinished implementations; their deletion does not establish removal of working search or bookmark synchronization. Storyboard/project changes are inventory-only evidence; no runtime verification is supplied. |
| [#89 — Swift4](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/89) **U** 2018-02-02 | maintenance / I | CLOSED-UNMERGED: not an implemented fix. Proposed Swift 4 migration with broad application, persistence, reader, UI, dependency and project changes. Caveat: Unmerged. Title suggests toolchain migration, but file scope is much broader; no exact feature or successful build can be inferred. |
| [#90 — Kiwix for iOS 1.9](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/90) **M** 2018-02-02 | platform-ui / D | Broad iOS 1.9 reader/library rewrite, not merely release or branding churn. The diff selects WKWebView with a kiwix URL-scheme handler on iOS 11+, retaining UIWebView on older iOS; serves ZIM bytes with MIME type and length; opens HTTP(S) navigation in SFSafariViewController and cancels geo/other non-Kiwix navigation. It adds adaptive bookmark/contents popovers, a date-descending bookmark table with swipe-to-unbookmark, bookmark feedback HUDs, and live font-size adjustment. Search combines up to 20 embedded-index results with per-book title suggestions, deduplicates by URL, ranks by title edit distance weighted by search probability, and provides book-selection and recent-query controls. Library UI becomes onboarding followed by a split-view layout with downloads, on-device books, categories, language filtering and metadata/action details. Downloads use one background session with explicit Wi-Fi-only or cellular-enabled requests and queued/downloading/paused/error states; completed files move into Documents and become local through scanning. Launch, foreground entry and monitored directory changes trigger scans accepting .zim/.zimaa files. Catalogue refresh removes absent cloud entries without deleting non-cloud books. Core Data changes consolidate download progress into Book and add migration state/category conversion. The diff removes background catalogue fetch, local notifications, quick-action/custom-URL opening handlers, bookmark Spotlight/widget updates, and history/notification settings. Caveat: Marked merged, but no runtime/build verification is supplied; body and comments are empty. Several contracts are incomplete or contradicted by code: category browsing compares numeric stateRaw with the string 'cloud'; Delete Bookmarks has an empty implementation; Delete File promises bookmark retention, but scanning no longer preserves missing local books as retained and may delete their records. An empty search selection defaults to all readers, so deselecting every book does not disable search. External-index search is not called by SearchProcedure, and its bridge loop never advances after deleting a result. LegacyWebController calls injected helpers without injecting Inject.js. Recent queries are written only on termination, and overflow trimming uses the old array, potentially discarding the newly added query. Bookmark changes have no immediate explicit save. Download-error detail actions are not implemented; file-move failures are ignored. macOS retains references to deleted ZimManager and obsolete search/result symbols, so cross-platform compatibility is not established. New toolbar code does not restore the old explicit accessibility labels. Store mappings, storyboard wiring, project configuration and translations are represented only by file lists, limiting verification of integration and migration. |
| [#109 — iOS 1.9 bug fixes](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/109) **M** 2018-02-14 | platform-ui / D | Mixed iOS feature/fix patch: replaces the bookmark widget with a four-item display backed by up to eight recent bookmarks in shared defaults, and adds kiwix: URL handling to load articles. Adds a Bookmark home-screen action and publishes a Continue Reading action. Adds a backup switch controlling backup-exclusion flags for top-level Documents entries with extensions containing 'zim' or equal to 'idx'; scans reapply this preference. Saves Core Data changes on backgrounding as well as termination, and persists recent searches when changed. Integrates external indexes into full-text result retrieval, preferring embedded indexes and looking for external indexes at &lt;stem&gt;.zim.idx. Catalog categories no longer restrict results to cloud books; book details distinguish embedded/external indexes, show an eight-character ID, and move destructive actions below metadata. File deletion expands to sibling names containing the selected file's stem. Also widens file-size arithmetic to 64 bits, normalizes HTML MIME responses, and adjusts search queuing, snippets, hit testing and iOS layout. Caveat: Merged status is not runtime verification. Continue Reading and Search shortcut handlers are no-ops despite Continue Reading being published. The widget update method calls its completion handler twice and compares only URLs when detecting changes. Bookmark-deletion handling remains empty. Sibling deletion uses substring matching, not an exact multipart/index association, and can target other similarly named entries. Backup flag errors are ignored. Schema, storyboard and project changes are listed without contents, so their wiring and migration correctness are not established. No body or comments supply additional validation. |
| [#116 — iOS 1.9 patch](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/116) **M** 2018-02-19 | platform-ui / D | Changes bookmark and table-of-contents presentation from popovers to a toggled side panel at regular horizontal size and a navigation-wrapped modal at compact size. Repeatedly tapping the active panel's button hides it; tapping the other switches its content. Updates table-of-contents content after page loads while its panel is active, handles size-class transitions, and immediately deselects selected bookmark/content rows. Legacy UIWebView loads now evaluate bundled Inject.js when readable before notifying the delegate. Renames CoreDataContainer to PersistentContainer and extracts existing catalog numeric parsing into helpers without changing those conversions. Disables the Bookmark home-screen action's actual bookmark presentation. Caveat: Merged status is not runtime verification; panel storyboard wiring is represented only by the changed-file list. The Bookmark shortcut still reports success after its presentation call is commented out; Search and Continue Reading remain no-ops. The added LibraryRefreshProcedureNew is incomplete: its XML element callback is empty, it does not save parsed books or finish on the data-success path, and no supplied call site activates it; the existing refresh procedure remains. Inject.js contents are not supplied, so evaluating it does not establish any particular reader feature or security behavior. No body or comments supply additional validation. |
| [#124 — 1.9 patch](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/124) **M** 2018-04-10 | reader-security / D | Adds an iOS External Link setting, saved on leaving the settings screen: default Always ask offers Load/Cancel before opening HTTP(S) links in Safari view; Always load opens directly; Never load blocks opening but displays an informational alert. Resolves internal ZIM redirects through Entry.getFinalEntry(), and serves content with HTTP 200 responses carrying MIME type and length. Also adds Search and Bookmark home-screen shortcut handling, safe-area layout adjustments, and decimal formatting for article/media counts. Caveat: Merged historical diff, not runtime verification. Scope substantially exceeds the redirect-only body. The Never load and don't ask label contradicts the informational alert still shown. LegacyWebController returns false even for nonredirected internal URLs, blocking their navigation; WebKit allows them. macOS work is incomplete: book restoration/opening, main-page loading, search execution and result-cell rendering are commented out, while startup switches to a largely unwired replacement storyboard. Dependency/build changes do not establish a working macOS migration. |
| [#125 — redirection handling with new version of libkiwix](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/125) **M** 2018-05-06 | maintenance / D | Libkiwix compatibility update: archive content lookup uses getEntryFromPath, and redirect resolution still follows getFinalEntry but obtains the destination with getPath and ensures a leading slash. Content-length metadata uses an unsigned 64-bit number instead of unsigned int. The legacy web controller allows a previously rejected navigation branch. Also changes the book-detail label from 'Indexed' to 'Index' and bumps app/widget builds from 12 to 16. Caveat: Marked merged; runtime correctness and present availability are not verified. The supplied diff changes dependency references but does not show dependency contents or an exact libkiwix version. It removes WikiMed's static-library links without showing replacements for that target. Limited navigation context prevents specifying exactly which requests the newly allowed legacy branch covers. No body or comments provide additional validation. |
| [#128 — 1.9.1](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/128) **M** 2018-05-29 | persistence / D | Reworks iOS library, download state, search inclusion and bookmarks around Realm. On first migration, scans local .zim/.zimaa files, copies Core Data bookmarks whose ZIM records exist and saved language visibility, then refreshes the catalog. Bookmark lists are newest-first and support opening and individual deletion. Download details now offer Cancel for error states; cancellation resets the record to cloud and clears saved progress/resume data. Catalog refresh updates existing metadata, adds Wikiquote/Wikisource/Wiktionary categories and defaults the first refresh's language filter to the current locale. Search clearing cancels queued work; recent searches are deduplicated and capped at 20, and hidden results expire after 60 seconds. Also enables NSAllowsArbitraryLoads in the iOS transport-security configuration and bumps app/widget metadata to 1.9.1 build 18. Caveat: Merged evidence, not runtime verification. Core Data remains for migration, so the body's database-replacement claim is not complete removal. Migration skips bookmarks without matching scanned ZIM records, swallows errors and marks migration complete regardless; preservation of all prior bookmarks/download settings is not established. The claimed embedded-index detection fix is not demonstrated: metadata still derives hasEmbeddedIndex from _ftindex tags, and details no longer directly check the local reader for an embedded index. Bulk bookmark deletion remains an empty handler despite confirmation text; file deletion promises bookmark retention, but scanning can delete remote-less ZIM records. The category getter incorrectly reads stateRaw instead of categoryRaw. Pause still writes resume data to preferences while resume reads Realm data, with Realm population relying on the error callback. No comments or tests establish these paths' success. |
| [#131 — iOS 1.9.2](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/131) **M** 2018-06-20 | search / D | Adds Settings &gt; Search &gt; Snippet, persisting an exclusion preference and passing it through indexed search for both primary and external searchers. Disabling snippets skips snippet extraction and returns an empty snippet string. Also removes persistence of resume data produced during download cancellation. Caveat: Merged, but no runtime verification supplied. Geo-link callbacks and a map controller are added, but the presentation code is commented out: geo links remain cancelled without displaying a map. The settings text suggests a performance benefit; no measurements establish it. |

Launch restores non-stale bookmarks. Enables queued article search with progress/no-result states and title-only or snippet rows. Web loading resolves internal redirects and stops non-Kiwix navigation, including HTTP(S). Content retrieval returns nil for additional standard exceptions. Caveat: Merged beta changes, not runtime verification. Despite the crash-recovery comment, clearing zimFilePaths does not clear zimFileBookmarks, which launch still restores. Preference keys are renamed without a migration shown. Moving the snippet preference key into shared code does not itself add a macOS snippet-settings UI. |

| [#149 — iOS 1.9.6](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/149) **M** 2019-05-10 | catalog / D | Adds iOS 11+ catalog title search using case- and diacritic-insensitive substring matching, respecting selected languages, grouping results by language, and opening book details. XML parse success is unchecked and the previous parse-error handler is removed, allowing incomplete input to drive catalog deletions and refresh timestamps. The new Library settings controller is not listed in the settings menu, so its refresh/language controls are not reachable through the shown menu. Separate refresh-tracking properties mean its refreshing indicator does not track all refresh entry points. Onboarding error alerts remain TODO; WikiMed code is boilerplate, not a demonstrated reader. |

| [#160 — iOS 1.9.7](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/160) **M** 2019-07-08 | search / D | Replaces ProcedureKit search and library work with OperationQueue-based execution. New searches cancel queued searches; indexed searches allocate and delete a searcher per run and use only embedded indexes, with external-index availability always false. Library scans and refreshes run serially. Adds explicit preference defaults and readable-width search-result margins; Settings' Refresh Now action becomes a no-op. Caveat: Merged, not runtime-verified. Refresh errors are swallowed without setting the new error property: background fetch consequently reports failure, and failed onboarding refreshes cannot re-enable the download button through the shown error check. Refresh deduplication is removed. Search cleanup can delete a null or previously deleted searcher when cancellation occurs before initialization; the pointer is not reset after deletion. Empty body supplies no release guarantees. |
| [#161 — Files app support](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/161) **M** 2019-08-05 | import-storage / D | Adds a Library '+' document picker and file-URL import sheet showing filename and size, with copy, move, and, when allowed, open-in-place actions. Copy/move target Kiwix Documents; external files are tracked using persisted URL bookmarks and security-scoped access. External library entries offer Unlink rather than file deletion; unlink removes reader access and clears or deletes the library record without deleting the source file. Registers the .zim document type and opening-in-place support. Caveat: Merged evidence, not runtime verification. Both scanning and reader admission drop .zimaa support. Copy/move failures are only printed and the sheet dismisses, with no user-facing error. Bookmark restoration returns early on an unresolvable entry. Documents membership is inferred by matching filename existence, not full path. The project references the new controller through an absolute developer-machine path. The diff also removes an empty bookmark-deletion conditional; it does not establish working bookmark deletion. |

Metadata-read failure is presented as possible corruption without distinguishing other causes. Security-scoped access ends before metadata extraction from the newly constructed reader. No body or comments establish broader import validation guarantees. |

The new sidebar MapController shares its class name with the unchanged relocated MapController; target membership is not inspectable to resolve possible duplication. |

| [#238 — OPDS](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/238) **M** 2020-04-28 | catalog / D | Replaces the legacy library XML refresh with libkiwix OPDS parsing from https://library.kiwix.org/catalog/root.xml. Refresh adds catalog entries, removes absent remote-only entries, preserves on-device entries, and optionally updates existing metadata; the first successful refresh selects the current locale's language. iOS replaces catalog onboarding and pull-to-refresh with category empty-state refresh controls and a Catalog panel offering Update Now, last-update time, and an Auto updates switch. Fetching permits cached data. The added SwiftUI settings/backup screen is a placeholder, not the UIKit panel opened by navigation. Migration does not copy remoteURL to downloadURL, and metadata updates omit name. File scanning reverses the previous external-file bookmark guard, potentially affecting open-in-place persistence. UIKit-dependent LibraryService is also added to the macOS target without conditional imports. The changelog claims a font-preview height fix, but the supplied diff shows no corresponding font-preview implementation change. |

Cancellation now resets stored state only through a matching task's completion callback; without a matching task it returns without resetting state. File-move and database errors remain suppressed. |
| [#297 — 1.13.3](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/297) **M** 2020-08-08 | search / D | Replaces separate SwiftUI and legacy search filters with one UIKit interface: users toggle on-device archives, select all, reuse recent queries, and confirm before clearing history. Caveat: History selection sets the search-bar text; this diff alone does not establish automatic query execution. A restored task without a description exits restoration before the heartbeat starts. Caveat: The added-bookmark HUD appears before asynchronous persistence succeeds; parsing/storage failures are silently ignored, and creation has no duplicate check. This filters catalog refreshes rather than implementing service-worker support or deleting downloaded files. The changed existing-record branch falls through to record creation when updateExisting is false; the supplied diff ends before showing how that creation is saved. A duplicate hasVideos metadata read is also introduced. |
| [#368 — Zimfile detail](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/368) **M** 2021-04-03 | catalog / D | Replaces the iOS 14 ZIM-detail placeholder with metadata, download progress/error display, cellular-download preference, download/pause/resume/cancel actions, main-page opening and confirmed delete/unlink actions. Renames the persisted ZIM primary key from id to fileID with a Realm version-4 migration. Catalog refresh no longer attempts to insert an existing entry when updateExisting is false. Caveat: Merged, but not runtime-verified. Older iOS and the download-list route retain the legacy detail controller. Disk-space eligibility is computed only at initialization. Delete/unlink checks for a matching filename in Documents rather than proving the reader URL is inside Documents, so its unlink-not-delete promise has a filename-collision edge case; errors are swallowed. Unrelated incomplete changes replace the experimental SwiftUI search screen with literal text, introduce an unexplained cancelfileIDSearch call and retain stale identifier/API references, including macOS remove. |
| [#369 — Library language filter view](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/369) **M** 2021-04-04 | catalog / D | Adds an iOS 14 language-filter popover with localized language names, database-wide file counts, Showing/Hiding sections, tap-to-toggle persisted language selections, and persisted alphabetical or descending-count sorting. Caveat: Merged, but incomplete and not runtime-verified. The newly routed iOS 14 category screen contains only a placeholder, so this PR does not establish working filtered category results. It also removes experimental library category navigation links and restores legacy detail navigation in LibraryCategoryController. |
| [#370 — Library category view](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/370) **M** 2021-04-05 | catalog / D | Implements the iOS 14 category list using the selected language codes and category, reacting to preference and Realm collection changes. Groups rows by localized language name, sorts languages alphabetically and files by title then descending size, displays favicon and size/date/article-count summaries, and opens ZIM details on selection. Caveat: Merged, but not runtime-verified. An empty language selection yields no rows, and languages without a localized name are omitted. Removes the earlier experimental library and grouped-category views; the feature-flagged SwiftUI library-button branch is left empty, so that branch no longer presents a library. The older grid cell's favicon is also commented out. |
| [#372 — Library sidebar view](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/372) **M** 2021-04-14 | catalog / D | Routes the Library button on iOS 14+ to a new split-view library without a feature flag. Its sidebar lists on-device files and queued, active, paused or failed downloads, ordered by creation date ascending, plus all categories. Selections open file details or language-filterable category lists; Wikipedia is selected initially. Caveat: Merged source, not runtime verification. Info remains a placeholder. The add button only presents a ZIM document picker; this controller supplies no selection/import handler. Home-view file cells are commented out, while the home feature remains disabled. Older iOS retains the legacy library. |
| [#373 — Library search result view](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/373) **M** 2021-04-15 | catalog / D | Adds an iOS 14+ library search bar that queries stored ZIM titles using case- and diacritic-insensitive substring matching, restricted to selected library languages, after a 250 ms debounce. Result selection opens file details; a nonempty query with no matches displays 'No Results'. Caveat: Merged source, not runtime verification. This searches catalog titles, not article content, and applies no file-state restriction. Background queries have no stale-result rejection, and empty text is still queried rather than explicitly clearing results. |
| [#374 — Favicon download processor](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/374) **M** 2021-04-18 | catalog / D | Fetches missing favicons for category and library-search entries and writes returned data to matching Realm records in batches of five or after a nominal 0.5-second quiet period. Library search switches to observed results ordered by creation date ascending and a 0.5-second text-stability delay, removing the no-results message. Sidebar and detail views share queued, downloading, paused and error status rendering. Caveat: Merged source, not runtime verification. Downloads do not validate HTTP status or image data and have no in-flight deduplication. Delayed cache flushes bypass the callback semaphore, so cache synchronization is incomplete; database errors are swallowed. Paused status now reads 'Pause'. Search results begin without a filter until update is called. |
| [#375 — Library info view](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/375) **M** 2021-04-22 | catalog / D | Replaces the library Info placeholder with manual catalog refresh, refresh-in-progress disabling, last-update text, an auto-update preference that adjusts the background-fetch interval, and a persisted backup toggle. Backup changes and library scans apply backup exclusion flags to matching top-level document-directory ZIM files. Caveat: Merged source, not runtime verification. The explanatory text promises updates when opening the library, but this diff adds no opening-time refresh hook; requesting background fetch does not guarantee execution. Backup handling excludes opened-in-place files, stops explicitly handling .idx files, and silently ignores errors. |
| [#378 — removing old swiftui experimental code](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/378) **M** 2021-05-14 | maintenance / D | Removes the old experimental SwiftUI reader, home, outline and article-search implementation while relocating reusable UI helpers. Also changes the library sidebar's on-device and download lists to size-descending order and renders Info last-update values in secondary text color. Caveat: Merged source, not runtime verification. Despite the cleanup title, the diff includes observable library ordering and styling changes. Deleting experimental reader/search code does not establish removal of those capabilities from the separate production controllers. |
| [#380 — Search results view](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/380) **M** 2021-05-27 | search / D | Selects a new SwiftUI-backed article-search results controller on iOS 13+, retaining the legacy controller below iOS 13. Searches use included on-device files and rerun when text or inclusion changes. The UI provides per-file and All/None filters, progress and empty states, title/snippet results, and up to 20 deduplicated recent queries saved when opening a result. Recent queries reopen through kiwix://search URLs; clearing history asks for confirmation on iOS 14+ but clears immediately on iOS 13. Caveat: Merged source, not runtime verification. The SwiftUI library introduced earlier is now gated off by a false feature flag, restoring the legacy library by default. All/None writes inclusion flags to every stored file, not just displayed on-device files. Recent-query URLs are constructed without explicit escaping. Regular-width filter hosting does not explicitly inject its required environment object, leaving that path unverified. |
| [#381 — Coredata stack](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/381) **M** 2021-06-14 | persistence / D | Adds a parallel Core Data catalog-write path during OPDS refresh on iOS 13+: UUID-addressable metadata is batch inserted, and absent remote entries are batch deleted, while existing Realm processing remains. Metadata initialization now fails when required date, size, counts, creator or publisher cannot be read, rather than treating those fields as optional. Caveat: Merged source, not runtime verification or a completed persistence migration. The container actually used by refresh is configured with /dev/null, so this does not establish durable catalog storage. Non-UUID IDs are skipped; no Core Data UI read path or Realm migration is shown. Batch-insert update semantics depend on schema details not supplied as a diff. Stricter metadata requirements can reject entries previously accepted with missing fields. |
| [#383 — Library operations](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/383) **M** 2021-06-22 | catalog / D | Reworks catalog refresh and local-file scans as Realm upserts: refreshes now update existing metadata without a separate updateExisting option, remove obsolete remote entries, and retain on-device/downloading entries. Scans refresh local metadata and mark opened files on-device; missing files become remote when downloadable or are deleted otherwise. Removes the parallel Core Data refresh path and persistence helper. Caveat: Merged; runtime behavior is not verified. Metadata-only changes no longer count toward hasUpdates. Both upsert dictionaries use groupId although the model property is groupID, and omit hasIndex. The new bookmark helper requires a same-named file in Documents, unlike the removed helper that excluded Documents files; external-only files can therefore receive no bookmark. |
| [#384 — Library download favicon](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/384) **M** 2021-06-24 | catalog / D | On iOS 13+, category and catalog-search views request missing favicons through a shared Combine pipeline that deduplicates URLs, batches responses in groups of five, and saves data to entries matching each response URL. Empty categories show language-selection guidance using a shared adaptive information view. Caveat: Merged; runtime behavior is not verified. A request failure terminates the merged publisher without recovery, and replacing the single retained subscription can cancel earlier downloads. Saving matches the response URL rather than the original request URL, so redirects can prevent association with catalog entries. |
| [#385 — Defaults upgrade](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/385) **M** 2021-06-29 | persistence / D | Moves settings and reactive SwiftUI bindings to Defaults serialization, migrates legacy language selections and refresh timestamps, and converts legacy enum values. Enables the SwiftUI-library feature flag. Library language sorting and recent searches use shared Defaults-backed state; download details use a new cellular-access preference defaulting to false. Caveat: Merged; runtime behavior is not verified. Migration runs on every launch without a completion guard and interprets external-link policy as an integer even after converting it to a string, risking reset to alwaysAsk. Language-sort migration reads the misspelled libraryLangucageSortingMode key. No migration from downloadUsingCellular to libraryDownloadUsingCellular is supplied. Lowering availability on two views alone does not establish complete iOS 13 compatibility. |
| [#386 — Realm schema v5 migration](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/386) **M** 2021-06-30 | persistence / D | Raises Realm schema version to 5 and makes creation date, size, article/media counts, creator, and publisher nonoptional. Migration preserves existing values and substitutes the migration-time date, zero counts/size, or empty strings for missing values. Displays formatted metadata without the former nil checks and removes the article/articles suffix from articleCountDescription. Scans stop overwriting bookmarks in their metadata upserts. Caveat: Merged; migration and UI behavior are not runtime-verified. Missing dates become synthetic current dates rather than unknown values. Download percentage calculations can divide by the newly defaulted zero size. This removes scan-time bookmark assignment, not the separate stale-bookmark refresh path. |
| [#389 — New library iOS 13 compatibility](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/389) **M** 2021-08-21 | platform-ui / D | Routes iOS 13 users to the flagged SwiftUI library and replaces several iOS 14-only APIs with compatible navigation actions, observed objects, and settings observers. Converts ZimFile properties to Realm @Persisted declarations. File details add category metadata and explicitly show 'Zim file does not exist.' when no matching record exists. Caveat: Merged; the compatibility claim is not runtime-verified. The diff also removes the library's import button/document-picker action and category language-filter button. It removes the search-result selection callback assignment, leaving result taps without controller navigation in this change. |
| [#390 — Library category view](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/390) **M** 2021-08-25 | catalog / D | Makes an empty selected-language list mean all languages in category listings and throttles category updates to 0.2 seconds. Replaces batched favicon publishers with per-file URLSession downloads, one connection per host, database saves by file ID, and up to three retries delayed five seconds when no HTTP 200 response is available. Caveat: Merged; runtime behavior is not verified. Category favicon requests still require languageCode IN the selected list, so selecting no languages displays all entries but requests none of their icons. Search favicon downloading is commented out. Completion handling checks HTTP status but not the supplied transport error; requests are not deduplicated and retry counters are not reset. |
| [#391 — LibrarySearchResultView](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/391) **M** 2021-08-26 | catalog / D | Catalog search sorts newest creation dates first, treats no selected languages as unrestricted, requests missing favicons, and dismisses the keyboard on upward dragging. Restores search-result selection navigation to file details. Renames the library Info popover to Settings with a gear icon, shortens main-menu labels, and supplies an iOS 13 empty-category icon fallback. Caveat: Merged; runtime behavior is not verified. Search remains catalog-title matching, not article-content search. Favicon requests are issued for every matching entry missing data without an in-flight deduplication check. |
| [#393 — Onboarding](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/393) **M** 2021-09-06 | catalog / D | Adds a Get Started catalog-download action when there are no on-device files and no previous refresh. Initial successful refresh requests local-language Wikipedia favicons and initializes the language filter. Opening an already-initialized library queues refresh when auto-refresh is enabled and its timestamp is over six hours old. Empty-category guidance distinguishes an undownloaded catalog from language filtering. Caveat: Merged; runtime behavior is not verified. The onboarding view model sets isRefreshing but never observes completion or resets it, so a failed initial refresh can leave its action disabled. The library-open refresh path lacks the current-operation guard used by the onboarding action. Users with on-device files but no catalog timestamp do not receive the Get Started section. |
| [#394 — Fix two search related issues](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/394) **M** 2021-09-08 | search / D | Skips search execution and snippet extraction for an empty search string. Builds recent-search deep links by appending the query as a URL path component instead of interpolating it into a URL string, accommodating spaces and other escapable characters. Caveat: Merged, not runtime-verified. The empty-string guard does not reject whitespace-only queries; the URL change specifically affects recent-search buttons. |
| [#395 — Document picker](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/395) **M** 2021-09-12 | import-storage / D | Adds a library plus button opening a single-selection document picker for org.openzim.zim files. A selected URL with readable ZIM metadata opens FileImportController; otherwise it opens a filename-based import alert. Library detail actions now initialize on appearance and update through Realm state-change notifications, retaining state-dependent download, open, delete/unlink, and cancellation actions. Caveat: Merged, but runtime behavior is not verified. The diff routes files into existing import handling; it does not establish successful copying, linking, or persistent access. Invalid state values now leave existing actions unchanged rather than clearing them. |
| [#396 — Fix library search](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/396) **M** 2021-09-13 | search / D | Replaces library search's observed-results mutation with a publishing view model. Searches match titles case- and diacritic-insensitively within selected languages, sort by title ascending then size descending, publish database updates to the UI, and request missing favicons where a favicon URL exists. First catalog refresh sets the current-language filter before fetching data and uses selected languages for Wikipedia favicon preloading. Caveat: Merged, but runtime behavior is not verified. Unlike the previous conditional language predicate, the new search always applies languageCode IN the selected codes, so an empty selection yields no matches. Publisher errors produce an empty result list. The initial language setting can occur even if the subsequent refresh fails. |
| [#397 — Refactoring iOS 15 prep](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/397) **M** 2021-09-14 | maintenance / D | Primarily a source-organization refactor: moves library/home files, extracts the unchanged settings controller and feedback-email handling into a separate file, and reorders unchanged SwiftUI helpers. Removes the already-true SwiftUI-library feature flag; library presentation remains selected by iOS 13 availability, with the legacy controller used on older systems. Caveat: Merged, but runtime behavior is not verified. Despite the iOS 15 preparation title, the supplied diff establishes no new iOS 15-specific user feature. |
| [#398 — Drop iOS 12](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/398) **M** 2021-09-15 | platform-ui / D | Removes iOS 12 startup, navigation-policy and UIKit library/search/settings fallbacks; routes library, search and settings through the existing SwiftUI-backed controllers unconditionally. Sets default-background scroll-edge appearances on iOS 15 navigation/toolbar bars, plain styling for category/search-result lists and primary-color search-result text. Feedback email composition tolerates a missing version string. Also reverses document-picker validation: nil ZIM metadata opens FileImportController, while non-nil metadata opens FileImportAlertController. Caveat: Marked merged, without runtime verification. The project-file contents are not supplied, so the exact deployment-target setting cannot be confirmed. The import-validation reversal is unrelated to the title and opposite to the previous acceptance branch; it may reject valid files and send invalid files into the import flow. Deleting legacy controllers does not establish removal of their features from the retained SwiftUI implementations. |
| [#400 — Library improvements](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/400) **M** 2021-09-19 | catalog / D | Adds explicit catalog-refresh and empty-category messages, a no-results message for nonempty catalog searches, shared title/description/icon rows with on-device indicators, and inset grouped library styling on iPad with iOS 14+. Category filtering now returns no files when no languages are enabled. Fetches missing favicons from category/search result subscriptions instead of preloading Wikipedia icons on first refresh; validates image data before saving and serializes favicon delegate operations. Caveat: Merged source evidence, not runtime verification. Row snapshots observe fileID changes rather than title, description, or state changes, so those fields are not explicitly subscribed for live refresh. |
| [#401 — Refactor](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/401) **M** 2021-09-19 | maintenance / D | Refactors search-filter rows to use ListRow while retaining conditional inclusion checkmarks; removes ZimFileCell and legacy Realm primary-key/index override methods, reorders helpers, and renames closure parameters. No new user-facing feature is established. Caveat: Merged, but behavioral equivalence and database compatibility are not runtime-verified by the supplied evidence. |
| [#402 — Schema change](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/402) **M** 2021-09-19 | persistence / D | Introduces Realm schema version 6 with directly persisted category/state enums, migrates their prior raw-string fields, and updates scanning, catalog refresh, library, and search predicates to the new field names. Caveat: Merged but explicitly reverted by PR #403. This is a historical schema-change attempt, not an enduring feature or verified migration. |
| [#403 — Revert "Schema change"](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/403) **M** 2021-09-19 | persistence / D | Reverts PR #402: restores schema version 5, persisted categoryRaw/stateRaw strings, computed enum wrappers, and raw-field queries; removes the version-6 migration. Caveat: Body and diff agree on the revert. No reverse migration for databases already upgraded to version 6 is supplied, and successful recovery is not demonstrated. |
| [#404 — Attempt to fix search filter](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/404) **M** 2021-09-19 | search / D | Makes Clear immediately erase recent search texts without confirmation on all supported iOS versions; removes the alert state and destructive-confirmation dialog previously used on iOS 14+. Caveat: Despite the search-filter-fix title, the supplied implementation changes recent-search clearing, not file-inclusion filtering. Merging does not establish that the underlying reported problem was fixed. |
| [#405 — Fix parser](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/405) **M** 2021-09-21 | search / D | Resolves redirects before extracting first-paragraph or first-sentence search snippets and replaces SwiftSoup parsing with Fuzi byte-data parsing. Snippets omit reference-class children, remove newlines, and preserve direct bold children; iOS 15+ renders attributed snippets. Bookmark titles and article outlines use the new parser API. Caveat: Merged without supplied runtime tests. Paragraph extraction now accepts nonempty whitespace-only output rather than trimming it before deciding whether a snippet exists. Geographic-coordinate extraction is commented out rather than ported. |
| [#406 — Search result view refactor & fixes](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/406) **M** 2021-09-24 | search / D | Moves live on-device file observation into the search-filter view, retains per-file inclusion toggles, and restricts All/None writes to on-device files instead of every catalog entry. Separately publishes on-device files keyed by ID for result icons and the nothing-to-search state, while included-file observations continue driving searches. Caveat: The body claims resolution of #392, but the supplied evidence does not describe that issue or verify the fix at runtime. Search-history clearing remains immediate. |
| [#408 — Outline view](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/408) **M** 2021-10-07 | platform-ui / D | Replaces the iOS outline controller with a SwiftUI-hosted heading list, trims heading text, normalizes heading levels, and treats a sole h1 as a title or centered list entry. Heading selection scrolls the article to that heading. Compact presentations gain medium/large sheets on iOS 15+, with code intended to keep a non-large sheet open after selection; overlay sidebars hide after selection. Caveat: Merged source, not verified presentation behavior. Sheet detents are configured on the navigation controller while selection checks the child controller's sheetPresentationController. The replacement also drops explicit multiline labels and heading-weight styling. |
| [#409 — Experimental dark mode css](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/409) **M** 2021-10-10 | platform-ui / D | Enables a feature flag that injects bundled dark-mode CSS at document end into the iOS reader's main frame and subframes. Under a dark color scheme, it changes page, link, table, infobox, and note colors. Caveat: Experimental styling; injection depends on loading the bundled CSS. Despite the Wikipedia-specific name, the implementation has no Wikipedia or category restriction. Merged status does not verify rendering across archives. |

Caveat: Unmerged and incomplete. Search is not bound to results, navigation/home/random buttons have empty actions, and the file-picker completion does nothing. It does not establish a functioning replacement macOS reader. |
| [#414 — Quicklook Preview](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/414) **M** 2021-10-19 | platform-ui / D | Adds an iOS Quick Look preview controller that opens a ZIM and loads its main page in a custom-scheme web view, rejects activated links, and reports invalid-file or navigation-completion results. Introduces typed URLContent retrieval and removes the Documents-directory existence restriction from file-bookmark generation. Caveat: Merged, but Quick Look registration and storyboard wiring are represented only by changed-file inventory, not inspected configuration. No provisional-navigation-failure callback is supplied, and successful system preview invocation is not demonstrated. |
| [#416 — Bookmark view](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/416) **M** 2021-10-30 | platform-ui / D | Adds a SwiftUI-hosted bookmarks view sorted newest first, with article titles, up to four snippet lines, thumbnail/favicon fallback, and empty-state instructions; selection opens the bookmarked URL. Outline loading now starts blank instead of immediately showing an unavailable message. Also expands dark CSS for tables, captions, sidebars, and images. Caveat: Merged without runtime verification. The supplied diff adds a BookmarksViewController but does not show removal of the pre-existing same-named controller from SidebarViewController.swift; project-file changes alone do not establish how that overlap is resolved. Bookmark selection has no explicit sheet dismissal in the new callback. |
| [#417 — iOS 1.15](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/417) **M** 2021-10-31 | platform-ui / D | Makes bookmark thumbnails 18-point fill-cropped images and fallback icons 18 points, adds configurable favicon sizing/content mode, and converts Bookmark properties to Realm @Persisted declarations. Removes the AppDelegate foreground-triggered library scan and its search/bookmark home-screen quick-action handler. Caveat: The release title does not make this release-metadata-only: the diff changes UI, persistence declarations, and lifecycle behavior. No replacement foreground scan or quick-action handler is supplied here; removal does not prove those capabilities are absent elsewhere. Runtime and release delivery are unverified. |
| [#419 — macOS swiftUI](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/419) **M** 2021-11-06 | platform-ui / D | Adds a partial macOS SwiftUI app with ZIM file selection that queues a library scan, an on-device library list that opens archive main pages, custom-scheme web rendering, back/forward controls, tabs, sidebar switching, and article-title updates. Restricts the custom Realm file-location move/configuration to iOS, leaving macOS on Realm's default location. Caveat: Merged but incomplete: search results, bookmarks, and outline are placeholders; Scope, home, and random actions are empty; the archive subtitle is hardcoded; dark CSS injection is commented out. No macOS database relocation or reader-reopening path is shown. This does not establish replacement of the existing macOS app or runtime readiness. |

Relocates shared URL/content helpers and on-device queries; deletes the iOS AppDelegate URL-opening handler and UIKit bookmark sidebar implementation. Caveat: Despite the refactoring title, the diff changes database-path and legacy-upgrade handling and removes an external URL/file-opening entry point without showing a replacement. Default-configuration initialization is not established by this diff. Merged status does not verify runtime behavior. |
| [#424 — Try catch random page function](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/424) **M** 2021-12-07 | platform-ui / D | Returns nil when random-page retrieval throws a caught C++ standard exception. Shows an Error alert with retry/different-ZIM advice when no random-page URL can be obtained, rather than silently returning. Caveat: Merged, not runtime-verified. The catch covers std::exception, not every possible exception. The alert also covers having no eligible ZIM file, not just the NoEntry failure described in the body. |
| [#425 — Use Debug in feature flag](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/425) **M** 2021-12-12 | platform-ui / D | Changes the Wikipedia dark user-CSS feature flag from always enabled to enabled only when compiled with DEBUG; non-DEBUG builds return false. Caveat: The diff establishes flag values, not successful CSS injection or visual results. Merged status is not runtime verification. |
| [#426 — macOS swift UI](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/426) **M** 2021-12-14 | platform-ui / D | Extends the macOS SwiftUI interface with startup library scanning, selectable on-device books showing sizes, debounced search across included books, result-to-reader navigation, main-page and random-page actions, history controls, and sidebar/navigation keyboard commands. Reader navigation resolves kiwix redirects, cancels non-kiwix navigation, and expands HTML details elements after loading; dark CSS injection is feature-gated. Caveat: Bookmark and table-of-contents panes remain placeholders. Search All/None buttons have empty actions, and recent searches are displayed without replay actions. Home uses the current URL's book and has no first-book fallback. This is partial implementation evidence, not verified macOS feature completeness. |
| [#427 — Search refactoring](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/427) **M** 2021-12-14 | search / D | Raises the full-text search request limit from 15 to 25, resolves title and full-text result paths to final entries before set-based aggregation, and disables snippets outside iOS while retaining the iOS snippet preference. Computes a per-reader title-result allowance based on a nominal total of 35. Caveat: The supplied diff passes the computed allowance into the title-search method but does not show it being used to limit suggestions; a hard 35-result cap is not established. Merged status does not verify result counts, deduplication outcomes or runtime stability. |

Widget project/resource changes are supplied only as a changed-file inventory; existing shared-defaults data is not explicitly cleared. |
| [#432 — macOS SwiftUI](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/432) **M** 2021-12-27 | persistence / D | Moves the macOS SwiftUI interface toward Core Data with persistent ZIM/bookmark entities and context-backed search-inclusion controls. Heading-visibility messages are registered but not handled, and observeHeadings is never called: scroll-synchronized outline selection is incomplete. The 35-result target is not a hard limit. macOS main/random-page actions now force-unwrap the current URL host as a UUID when no file ID is supplied, risking a crash for missing or invalid hosts. Redirect-path handling also drops the previous explicit leading-slash normalization and unchanged-path check. |
| [#447 — Swiftui components](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/447) **M** 2022-06-06 | platform-ui / D | Introduces shared SwiftUI iOS/macOS reader, library and search components, replacing macOS-specific views. iOS uses outline/bookmark sidebars at regular widths and sheets at compact widths; tapping the bookmark button shows bookmarks, while long-pressing toggles the current bookmark. Library views expose categories, recent catalog entries, metadata, download controls and URL/ID copying. The shared importer opens and records one ZIM at a time, replacing macOS multiple selection; bookmark creation now accesses security-scoped resources and uses minimal bookmarks on iOS. Adds background downloads with progress persistence, pause/resume/cancel, and destination moves to macOS Downloads or iOS Documents. macOS sidebar shortcuts become Command-1 through Command-4 for search, bookmarks, outline and library. Caveat: Merged, but no runtime verification is supplied. Several paths are incomplete: download completion only attempts a file move, silently ignores move errors and never opens/registers the downloaded ZIM; Settings presents an empty view; iOS HTTP(S) navigation is canceled with only a placeholder comment for an alert. Delete merely calls unlink, contradicting its comment and confirmation promising physical file deletion; neither action explicitly removes the article bookmarks promised by the alerts. Category, New and library Opened queries are hard-coded to English; Opened/New are empty below iOS 15. The macOS recent-search interface and updates are removed, and category grids no longer provide name search. Catalog batch-operation error paths can resume checked continuations twice. Generated project/schema changes are listed without diffs, so target integration and schema compatibility are not established. |

| [#454 — SwiftUI Apps](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/454) **M** 2022-07-28 | platform-ui / D | Expands the SwiftUI iOS/macOS apps with platform-specific reader layouts, settings and About screens. macOS gains reader/library window commands, sidebar/search/navigation shortcuts, page-zoom commands and search submission that opens the first result. iOS gains a settings sheet, animated outline/bookmark sidebars and web-view safe-area inset handling. Both readers handle incoming file URLs by opening the ZIM and selecting its main page, and accept kiwix article URLs. File import permits multiple selections; completed downloads invoke the same file-opening path. Library category/new views use persisted language selections instead of English-only filtering, exclude service-worker-requiring ZIMs and show empty states; New covers the preceding calendar month instead of 14 days. Language selectors expose localized names, file counts and sorting. Opened-file views retain missing entries with warning indicators and Locate/Unlink actions, while welcome/search-filter views exclude missing files. Detail screens warn that service-worker ZIMs are unsupported and offer main/random-page context actions for available files. Catalog refresh moves into LibraryViewModel with manual refresh, an automatic one-hour freshness gate, last-refresh display and iOS background-task registration. iOS backup controls update backup-exclusion flags for matching Documents files. Bookmark creation stores a first-sentence snippet and first-image URL when parsing succeeds. Caveat: Marked merged, but no runtime verification is supplied. No body or comments establish additional claims. Several settings are incomplete in the shown implementation: external-link policy and search-snippet selection are persisted without demonstrated consumption, and Download still explicitly disallows cellular access despite the cellular toggle. Refresh catches and suppresses errors/cancellation, then records a refresh timestamp even after failure; processing also has a possible double continuation resume on error. Existing catalog entries are not refreshed by the insert/delete-only processing. Background execution is requested, not guaranteed, and no repeat scheduling after execution is shown. Missing-file detection throws only when bookmark resolution fails; the underlying URL open is not shown reporting failure. Locate launches the general importer rather than a targeted replacement workflow. Delete/unlink confirmation is selected by a Documents basename-existence check, so even a macOS action labeled Unlink can produce Delete confirmation. Alert text promises removal of linked bookmarks, but the supplied changes do not independently prove that cleanup. Page-zoom changes are observed, but initial application of the saved zoom is not explicit. Generated project/schema changes are inventory-only evidence, not proof of target integration or migration correctness. |
| [#459 — iOS 15 + macOS 12](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/459) **M** 2022-09-22 | platform-ui / D | Reworks the SwiftUI app into a shared root: macOS combines reading, bookmarks and library sections in one window with New Tab and section shortcuts; iOS uses a search-bar reader with library/bookmark/settings sheets and outline menus or sheets. Search results open through the current URL binding, display snippets on macOS as well as iOS, and record deduplicated recent queries when a result is selected. Bookmarks become newest-first searchable cards, matching title or snippet, with View/Remove context actions and welcome-screen entries. Category grids gain name filtering. Startup reopens stored ZIM references and scans/monitors Documents for top-level .zim files. Adds an iOS-triggered Realm-to-Core Data migration for ZIM metadata and linked bookmarks, and WebView interaction-state preservation across view reconstruction. Catalog refresh moves to a serial operation queue with cached-data-first retrieval and updates the refresh timestamp only after successful processing. Download details add a cellular preference and a free-space confirmation. Caveat: Merged, but no runtime verification is supplied. The title does not establish deployment minimums from the inventory-only project changes; the new ReadingView requires iOS 16/macOS 12, while the active iOS root uses a separate UIKit wrapper. NavigationSplitView code remains commented out; Map is absent from the selectable sidebar items, and the standalone onboarding catalog button has an empty action. Delete still only calls unlink and never removes the physical file, contradicting its confirmation text; explicit bookmark removal on unlink is also absent, so cascade behavior cannot be established from the schema inventory. The purported 1GB reserve uses Swift's XOR operator (^), not exponentiation, and Download Anyway forces cellular access off regardless of the preference. Migration skips records lacking required legacy fields, removes earlier schema repair steps, and can abort bookmark processing when a referenced ZIM is missing. Catalog retrieval no longer checks HTTP status. These changes do not demonstrate successful migration, downloading or platform compatibility. |
| [#460 — Revert "iOS 15 + macOS 12"](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/460) **M** 2022-09-22 | platform-ui / D | Rollback of #459, not a new iOS 15/macOS 12 feature. The diff replaces the shared SwiftUI root with platform-specific readers and a separate macOS Library window; restores reader sidebar modes and keyboard commands; uses one retained web view per reader; and shows iPad bookmarks/outlines in a sidebar versus sheets on compact iOS layouts. Search loses recent-query history, gains All/None file-inclusion controls, and becomes title-only on macOS with snippet extraction limited to iOS. Bookmarks become oldest-first lists without the removed search/context menus; Welcome loses its empty-library prompt and bookmark section. Realm configuration returns from schema 6 to 5, removing the Realm-to-Core-Data migration and its startup invocation; the shared startup directory scan/monitor wiring is also removed. Caveat: Merged revert of kiwix/apple#459; neither runtime verification nor present-day behavior is established. The title does not prove deployment-target changes because project changes are inventory-only. Restored SwiftUI Delete still only calls unlink: it does not delete the physical file despite its confirmation text. Bookmark relationships are removed, and the shown unlink code does not explicitly delete bookmarks despite the warning promising that. Catalog refresh swallows errors yet updates the last-refresh timestamp; its processing error path can resume a continuation twice. These incomplete paths must not be described as working deletion, migration, or reliable refresh features. |
| [#461 — iOS 15 + macOS 12](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/461) **M** 2022-09-22 | platform-ui / D | Cross-platform SwiftUI reader/library overhaul: macOS places Reading, Bookmarks and library sections in one sidebar instead of a separate library window; iOS uses library/bookmark/outline/settings sheets and size-dependent reader toolbars. Search gains reusable recent queries saved when selecting results, per-file inclusion toggles and macOS snippet extraction. HTTP(S) navigation follows ask/always/never preferences, opening approved links in iOS Safari sheets or the macOS external browser. Web views preserve interaction state when reconstructed; iOS text sizing uses CSS adjustment. Startup reopens registered files, scans and monitors Documents for top-level .zim files, and initiates iOS Realm-to-Core Data migration of file records and bookmarks. Catalog refresh becomes serialized operations with cached-response fallback, progress indication and success-only refresh timestamps. Download details add a cellular preference and space-warning confirmation; settings add feedback and review links. Caveat: Merged, but no runtime verification is supplied. The title alone does not establish deployment minimums; project/schema changes are inventory-only, and the iOS root actually uses the UIKit-hosted reader rather than the added iOS-16-only ReadingView. NavigationSplitView remains commented out, and the added Boston-centered map is not exposed in the sidebar. Delete still only calls unlink, contradicting its confirmation that the physical file will be deleted; unlink does not explicitly remove bookmarks when retaining a downloadable catalog record, and schema cascade behavior is not shown. The space threshold uses Swift XOR (10^9), not one billion; Download Anyway forces cellular access off despite the toggle. Returning iOS text zoom to 1 skips CSS adjustment rather than resetting it. Migration silently skips records missing required fields, removes earlier schema-repair logic, runs asynchronously and can stop bookmark processing on a missing file relation; preservation across older schemas is therefore not established.
