Adds iOS sidebar destinations for opened files, categories, downloads and new content, plus a compact-menu Library sheet. Centralizes library selection, preserving iOS detail navigation and macOS detail panels. The iOS scene URL-opening callback also creates a tab for a non-file URL. Caveat: Merged integration, not runtime verification. Non-file scene URLs are passed through without scheme validation in the shown callback; cold-launch URL handling is not established here. Library tab selection moves from SceneStorage to an in-memory view-model property, so persisted selection is no longer demonstrated. Tests shown only update the view-model name; they do not verify the new navigation contract. |
| [#510 — Settings in multitab setup](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/510) **M** 2023-07-22 | platform-ui / D | Makes settings reachable in the iOS multitab interface: a compact tabs-manager menu action opens a dismissible settings sheet, and the split-view settings destination displays settings in the secondary column. The iOS list contains existing reading/library controls, feedback, app-rating and About actions. macOS retains a 550-by-400 tabbed settings window. External-link policy labels change to title case. Caveat: Merged UI integration, not runtime verification. Most settings files are unchanged relocations; the diff does not add settings capabilities or change external-link enforcement. The policy change is label capitalization only. |
| [#512 — Search in multi tab setup 1/2](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/512) **M** 2023-07-27 | search / D | Adds a search view model per browser tab and overlays search results while the toolbar search is active. Widths above 700 points show filters and recent searches beside results; narrower views show the sidebar when the query is empty and results otherwise. Selecting a result records the query in recent history, posts an openURL notification and dismisses search, replacing the previous selection callback. Retains per-ZIM search toggles, All/None controls and six displayed recent queries; replaces the clear-history alert with a confirmation dialog. Tabs without a URL show a Welcome placeholder. Browser buttons are extracted without substantive behavior changes. Caveat: Merged, but runtime behavior is not verified. The title identifies this as part 1/2; no second part is supplied. SearchViewModel is moved unchanged, so this does not establish a new search algorithm. Result routing now depends on notification handling outside this diff; the legacy root also loses its direct URL assignment and search-state reset callback, so successful navigation there is not established. |
| [#513 — Split tabs & app structure](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/513) **M** 2023-08-17 | platform-ui / D | Replaces the UIKit iOS app shell with SwiftUI: iOS 16+ uses a split sidebar at regular width and a tab-manager sheet at compact width, with new-tab, close-tab and close-all actions; older iOS uses a single shared reader. Tabs retain cached web views, restore saved interaction state, and save cached states when the scene becomes inactive. Startup selects the tab with the latest lastOpened value. Article URL notifications load the selected tab or create one when a non-tab destination is selected. Also enables actual internal-link context-menu previews, adds an iOS main-page file picker menu, and moves external-link policy prompts into a shared modifier. Caveat: Merged, but no runtime verification is supplied. Tab-cache removal on close is commented out. lastOpened updates on article-title observation rather than explicitly on every tab selection. iOS incoming file URLs now go directly through reader URL loading rather than the removed scene delegate's LibraryOperations.open path; macOS still opens files but its former automatic main-page navigation is commented out. The feature-flagged map destination is removed from the macOS shell, and the enabled-map geo-link branch only parses coordinates without presenting a map. Compact onboarding still has a TODO to open catalog categories directly. Layout handling now resets web-view zoom to 1 after debounced layout changes rather than preserving the previous background/foreground zoom. |
| [#514 — Commands](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/514) **M** 2023-08-18 | platform-ui / D | Wires back/forward commands (Command-[ and Command-]) to the focused browser, disabling them when navigation is unavailable. Enables navigation and zoom command groups on iOS; retains macOS navigation/sidebar shortcuts and adds standard SidebarCommands. Zoom commands require a browser URL, change the saved zoom by 0.1, disable increases at &gt;=2 and decreases at &lt;=0.5, and reset to 1 with Command-0. Saved zoom changes update macOS pageZoom or iOS text sizing; iOS reapplies text sizing after page loads, including at 100%. Caveat: Merged, but no runtime verification is supplied. Zoom thresholds disable commands rather than clamp stored values. macOS observes zoom changes; this diff does not establish initial application of saved zoom to newly created web views. |
| [#515 — File open and import](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/515) **M** 2023-08-21 | import-storage / D | Centralizes ZIM opening from file URLs, the macOS Open command, onboarding, and the library picker. Successful opens register files in the library; command/file opens request each file's main page through NSWorkspace on macOS or a new tab on iOS 16+, while onboarding opens the main page through ordinary navigation and library imports do not navigate. Failed opens produce an alert naming the files. The reusable Open picker now permits only one file; the library picker retains multiple selection. Incoming non-file URLs are forwarded only for the kiwix scheme. Caveat: Merged, but no runtime verification is supplied. Comments explicitly leave iOS/iPadOS command-based file importing unsupported and cite multitab limitations as the reason for single selection. Explicit new-tab handling is shown only for iOS 16+; older-iOS new-tab behavior is not established. macOS delegates main-page opening to NSWorkspace rather than directly creating a tab. Core Data schema and Info.plist changes are listed without content, so migration and file-association details cannot be verified. |
| [#523 — Version 3.2](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/523) **M** 2023-09-27 | platform-ui / D | Replaces separate iOS legacy/regular navigation with a UIKit split controller and compact search controller, extending tab management to iOS 15. Provides new-tab, close-current, close-all and swipe-to-close actions; closing all creates a replacement tab. Adds Open in New Tab to iOS link menus; library main/random-page actions and download-notification taps request new tabs. Caches browser models per tab, restores saved web interaction state, and saves it when browser content disappears or cached models are purged. Memory warnings purge older cache entries only when more than ten exist, retaining five. Search dismisses on URL-opening notifications and navigation/article shortcuts. iOS outlines use a hierarchical popover with half-sheet adaptation; library sheet selection uses scene storage, and Welcome can open Categories directly. macOS URL notifications load only in the key window. Caveat: Merged, but no runtime verification is supplied. No body or comments establish additional promises or reversions. Becoming inactive now saves the database rather than explicitly snapshotting every web view, so this diff does not establish that the visible tab's latest interaction state is captured at backgrounding. macOS retains a single reading browser per window; this is not evidence of macOS tab management. |

The macOS reading view remains bound to its separate browser, and the existing URL handler still selects Reading, so the added observer does not establish working tab switching. Some tab buttons only perform diagnostics. The macOS listing references TabLabel although its declaration remains iOS-only, leaving an apparent compilation blocker. Placeholder labels/title and commented-out controls further demonstrate incompleteness. Issue 518 is referenced only by the PR title; its requirements and resolution are not supplied. |

| [#768 — Support range requests for video content](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/768) **U** 2024-08-02 | reader-media / D | CLOSED-UNMERGED: not an implemented fix. Unmerged proposal to read requested video byte ranges and return HTTP 206 responses with Content-Range and inclusive range lengths; non-video content retains HTTP 200. Also adds ZIM-file-date-based response validators and guards an optional JavaScript heading handler. Caveat: Closed in favor of PR 894 after requests to rebase and complete it. All video responses use 206, even without a Range header; range parsing is limited. The author's claim that correcting an off-by-one error eliminated tiny requests was contradicted by reviewer and later author observations of continuing MP4 eight-byte requests. Validator headers were acknowledged as ancillary. Submitted review: The PR lacks a description and includes unexplained date changes. Reviewer reports MP4 videos still trigger 8-byte requests on this branch; WebM did not exhibit that problem originally. All videos receive HTTP 206 even without a range request, which the reviewer identifies as noncompliant. |
| [#769 — Move video content loading off the main thread](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/769) **U** 2024-05-24 | reader-media / D | CLOSED-UNMERGED: not an implemented fix. Unmerged proposal to read ZIM content in detached utility-priority tasks and deliver WebKit responses on the main actor, avoiding synchronous content reads on the UI thread. Caveat: Marked draft pending video changes; comments point to separate implementation in PR 774. The supplied diff builds on the range-response proposal rather than being an independent deployed change. Body responsiveness claims are not established by merge or runtime evidence. |

| [#771 — [457] - Immersive reading view](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/771) **M** 2024-05-24 | platform-ui / D | Adds iPhone reader bar hiding while dragging below the drag-start offset, with bars restored above that offset, on URL changes, scrolling to top, and size transitions. Hiding extends the web view to the view's top edge. Caveat: Direction is measured against the drag-start offset, not the previous scroll event. Restoration applies to all size transitions, not just landscape rotation. Final guards exclude iPad despite an earlier review reporting iPad hiding. The diff also removes restoration/resize workarounds and uses private WebKit inset keys; side-effect checks are requested, not demonstrated. Submitted review: The body reports implementation changes rather than explicit test results: removal of an obsolete layout hack and showing bars on rotation, URL changes, and scrolling to the top. Concurrent scrolling and navigation motivated the extra safeguard. |
| [#774 — Move HTML content loading off the UI thread (version2)](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/774) **M** 2024-05-25 | reader-media / D | Moves ZIM content reads into detached utility-priority tasks while returning HTTP 200 or 404 responses through main-actor methods, reducing UI-thread blocking during large content loads. | [#778 — 773 streaming uncompressed data](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/778) **M** 2024-06-02 | reader-media / D | Streams directly accessible audio/video data from the archive file in 2 MiB chunks using utility tasks. Falls back to a single libzim read for other content or unavailable direct access, then delivers data through WebKit. Adds byte-range and stream unit tests. Caveat: Merged, with body explicitly noting unresolved scheme-closing synchronization and possible cancellation work. Compressed/libzim chunk streaming remains inactive pending libzim issue 886 and Apple issue 784. A comment says uncompressed content uses the full range, but the diff specifically chunks directly accessible media. Added tests do not establish end-to-end playback or resolution of issue 744. Submitted review: Initial review requires addressing URL-scheme closing synchronization and cancellation when leaving a loading page before adding range handling. Later positive reviews still mention a libzim issue, CI, and Git-history concerns without documenting their resolution. |

Caveat: Merged source change; this does not open internal links in a new iOS tab. PR 800 subsequently replaces that same-view behavior with new-tab dispatch. |
| [#790 — 784 stream data in chunks for compressed data via libzim](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/790) **M** 2024-06-05 | reader-media / D | Extends chunked loading to libzim-backed content larger than 2 MiB, retaining single reads for smaller content and direct-file chunks for eligible media. Fixes range reads to copy bytes from the requested blob rather than the start of the whole item. Caveat: Merged source change, not playback verification. This concerns internal chunk delivery, not HTTP Range/206 support; small fallback reads retain the full-range path. |
| [#791 — Fix macOS webView full-screen mode](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/791) **M** 2024-06-07 | reader-media / D | Wraps the macOS WKWebView in an NSView with edge constraints instead of returning WKWebView directly, addressing its fullscreen hosting/layout behavior. | [#894 — Support range requests](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/894) **M** 2024-08-02 | reader-media / D | Adds Range-header parsing and partial ZIM-resource streaming in chunks up to 2 MiB, returning HTTP 206 for parsed ranges and 200 otherwise. Range support is incomplete: suffix/open-ended and out-of-bounds ranges are not properly handled, some inputs can construct invalid ranges, and Content-Range reports an upper bound one greater than the bytes selected for streaming. The retained comment claiming 206 is skipped contradicts the implementation. Added tests cover chunk generation/streaming, not HTTP range compliance; no runtime confirmation supplied. |

Welcome navigates onward only after a complete refresh. Caveat: Merged without supplied runtime confirmation. Despite the 503-specific title, the state covers caught refresh errors generally. A non-HTTP response can still return nil without setting error or restoring the prior state. Test changes replace response URLs with mocks rather than adding a 503-specific test. Caveat: Merged on top of #956; no end-to-end migration verification supplied. Bookmark fetch/save errors are suppressed while the migration returns success, potentially marking incomplete work complete. External URL intake and OS registration now accept zim rather than kiwix; internal legacy conversion is not blanket external-link compatibility. The plist omission raised in review is addressed in the supplied diff. Replacing the older host-ID migration remains a proposed follow-up. |

| [#974 — 861 library loading is blocking the main thread](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/974) **M** 2024-09-21 | import-storage / D | The diff replaces eager reopening of saved ZIM archives with URL registration and cached archive opening on demand, and routes Swift ZIM-service access through a global actor. Library main/random-page actions and welcome tiles show loading indicators while awaiting access and ignore repeat activation while busy. Search explicitly opens selected searchable archives before searching. Download success handling also expands from HTTP 200 to all 2xx responses. Caveat: Merged historical change, not proof of current behavior or measured startup performance. The blanket claim that archives no longer open at startup is qualified by search initialization opening searchable archives. The actor isolation shown does not establish safety of every Objective-C++ archive access. Reopening now returns from the entire loop if a saved entry lacks bookmark data. Comments report a progress-indicator correction and visual approval; coverage is incomplete. Comments identify iPad landscape logo movement and no native macOS splash screen. Animated English loading text and spinner removal are requested requirements, not verified implementation. Submitted review: The launch-screen behavior and work performed during startup are unclear. On the reviewer's old, slow iPhone, the logo splash is visible but not especially noticeable compared with other apps. |

| [#979 — Remove duplicate entry from search results](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/979) **U** 2024-09-18 | search / D | CLOSED-UNMERGED: not an implemented fix. Unmerged duplicate-search-result proposal: records archive entry indices on indexed results, suppresses title results matching an existing index, and clears the retained result's probability to affect ranking. Caveat: Explicitly superseded by PR #981. Comparison checks entry index without archive identity, so it can confuse equal indices from different ZIMs. It only scans results present before each archive's title-search loop. Later discussion on #981 questions clearing probability as a ranking solution. No patch coverage was reported. |
| [#981 — Remove duplicate search entries](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/981) **M** 2024-09-18 | search / D | The diff deduplicates indexed and title search results using a set of URLs normalized with trailing slashes for comparison only, preserving original navigation URLs and the first encountered result. It searches each ZIM separately, requesting up to 25 indexed and 25 title results per archive, with separate exception handling so one search failure need not discard other archives' results. Caveat: Merged historical change superseding #979, not runtime verification. An intermediate comment claims title-first ordering, but the supplied diff and later summary use index-first ordering to retain snippets. Duplicate ranking and cross-platform search design remain unresolved, with follow-up #983. The per-archive result limits also change the prior pooled search allocation. |
| [#982 — Fix video only on active scene](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/982) **M** 2024-09-18 | reader-media / D | On iPad and macOS BrowserTab, the diff refreshes video state when scenePhase changes to active rather than on view appearance, avoiding that refresh from background appearance callbacks. Existing disappearance handling still pauses video outside picture-in-picture. Caveat: Merged historical change. The author reports testing on iPadOS 17 and macOS; iPhone is explicitly outside this view's scope. This is a change to the refresh trigger, not a general guarantee that all video playback occurs only in active scenes. |

Unit tests cover cache operations, not browser restoration equivalence. A comment reports failing CI; project.yml dependency edits are not supplied. |

| [#1000 — 975 improve iphone launch sequence new](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/1000) **M** 2024-10-10 | platform-ui / D | The diff adds an iOS logo-only launch storyboard, light/dark logo assets, and matching logo-based loading/welcome layouts with localized loading messages. Compact iOS navigation bars remain hidden during loading; the split view starts secondary-only. Library tabs become Categories, New, Downloads, Opened, with Categories as the default selection unless scene state restores another tab. Welcome actions are initially transparent until categories have been seen, catalog retrieval fails, or the upgrade fallback applies; catalog completion opens the library/categories. Actions stack vertically except in compact-height landscape. macOS adopts NavigationSplitView. Caveat: Marked merged, not runtime-verified. Reopens PR #976. Comments clarify that the spinner is for subsequent launches, not first-time users, consistent with its hasSeenCategories condition. The hasSeenCategories flag is also set when the entire library disappears, not strictly after viewing Categories. The body’s removal of splash text means a separate message: the new logo still contains the KIWIX wordmark. Submitted review: The logo/buttons block is not vertically centered, and iphone_post_inst_dark.mov still shows a spinner where one was explicitly ruled out. |

| [#1005 — Close Picture in Picture if the ZIM file is deleted/unlinked](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/1005) **M** 2024-11-01 | reader-media / D | The diff extends ZIM unlink/delete cleanup to evict browser models for deleted tabs, detach observers/delegates, suspend all WebView media playback, close media presentations including Picture in Picture, and stop loading. iOS creates a replacement tab when none remain, including when the last tab is deleted while another sidebar item is selected. macOS switches to tab-ID-keyed browser caching and resets affected windows’ stored tab IDs for replacement browser creation. Several iOS sheets/popovers migrate to NavigationStack. Caveat: Marked merged, not runtime-verified. The body explicitly leaves macOS window restoration broken (#1015) and browser-model memory retention unresolved (#1016), so cache eviction does not establish deallocation. macOS cleanup resets the stored tab ID without explicitly changing currentItem in that handler. Beyond the stated media fix, the diff removes the iOS createWebViewWith callback’s internal-URL open-in-new-tab notification and removes Reading from macOS sidebar navigation commands. The body incorrectly attributes the onBoarding-to-welcomeScreen rename to NavigationItem; the diff renames OpenFileContext. Added cache tests do not verify Picture in Picture behavior. |

Catalog apps select loading, fetching, welcome/error actions, or the local-ZIM/bookmark grid, and route to the catalog on qualifying refresh completion. Submitted review: Initial review reported three CodeFactor issues, a non-clickable Settings row outside its text, recurring-payment code and a visible Monthly tab, missing success/failure feedback, unexplained supportedNetworks/capabilities, and missing implementation references. Later approval explicitly says tested but does not enumerate coverage of all earlier concerns. |

Payment availability is initialized only when Brand.hideDonation equals false, whereas the previous rendering condition accepted any value other than true. |

The supplied section filters incorrectly retain excluded sections in three flag combinations, potentially leaving required snapshot sections absent; PR #1151 corrects this. Sidebar donation insertion also lacks the payment-availability check later added by #1187. Thus the body's intended conditional visibility is not fully supported by this diff. |

| [#1144 — Handle background downloads to update live activities](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/1144) **M** 2025-03-22 | platform-ui / D | Introduces a shared Live Activity service and registers a standard-app background refresh handler that starts the service and submits another refresh request with an earliest start two seconds later. Replaces the permitted library-refresh identifier with a download-Live-Activity identifier. Caveat: Merged, but the body overstates assured background updating. The supplied diff does not submit an initial refresh request outside the handler, repeatedly attempts registration on lifecycle callbacks, and does not complete the delivered task. PRs #1145 and #1146 revise this approach; the unmerged #1147 reports that BackgroundTask scheduling proved unreliable for this purpose. |
| [#1145 — Fix background mode scheduler for live activities](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/1145) **M** 2025-03-24 | platform-ui / D | Submits a download-Live-Activity background refresh request when the app enters the background, instead of registering again there. The registered handler starts the shared service, reschedules refresh and marks its task completed; scheduling is restricted to the standard app. Caveat: Merged, not evidence of reliable background progress delivery. The handler calls start rather than directly forcing a progress update. PR #1146 replaces this delegate-based scheduling, and unmerged #1147 reports the broader scheduling approach unsuccessful. |
| [#1146 — 1107 LiveActivities instant updates fixes](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/1146) **M** 2025-03-28 | platform-ui / D | Moves refresh handling to SwiftUI backgroundTask, schedules requests on background scene transitions with a three-second earliest start, and directly reads current download state to update or stop Live Activities. Removes application-side update throttling. Replaces the estimated timer-driven bar with measured byte progress while retaining a countdown or pause icon, and changes speed estimation to an arithmetic mean of rates measured from the oldest sample. Caveat: Merged, not runtime-verified; the review requests a subsequent TestFlight build rather than reporting test results. Three seconds is an earliest scheduling date, not a guaranteed refresh cadence. forceUpdate launches asynchronous update work without awaiting its completion. Unmerged #1147 reports background scheduling unsuccessful and proposes removing it and restoring estimated animation. |
| [#1147 — 1107 Live Activities](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/1147) **U** 2025-05-01 | platform-ui / D | CLOSED-UNMERGED: not an implemented fix. Unmerged proposal to remove background-refresh scheduling, restore timer-interpolated download bars without downloaded-byte labels, use a three-second speed-sampling window and inflate remaining-time estimates by 10%. Also proposes ending Live Activities on the termination callback and preserving Downloads navigation during startup. Caveat: Not merged; none of these changes establishes delivered behavior. The body reports suspended-app progress unavailable and earlier BackgroundTask attempts unreliable, contradicting assurances in earlier background-update PRs. Termination cleanup depends on receiving the termination callback, so the diff does not establish cleanup for every termination. A reviewer objects to interpolation beyond a few seconds between updates; the proposed timer bar has no such limit. Push-based updates are discussed only as an alternative, not implemented. |

Caveat: Merged; the author reports testing on iPhone, iPad and macOS, but this does not independently verify leak elimination. Review noted failing CI at one stage; supplied comments do not establish its final resolution. Some strong browser references remain in the diff, so complete leak removal should not be inferred. Submitted review: The reviewer says the large change is mostly beyond their understanding and may have unforeseen effects elsewhere. |

Submitted review: The new 'offline' status is ambiguous and contradicts the issue's requested 'paused' status. Whether to additionally explain the offline cause remains a design question. |

| [#1494 — Allow to download ZIM files to an arbitrary location in macOS](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/1494) **U** 2026-03-16 | acquisition / D | CLOSED-UNMERGED: not an implemented fix. Proposed macOS Downloads settings tab to choose a persistent custom folder, display its volume/free space, and reset to ~/Downloads. Custom-folder downloads stream directly to destination files using buffered data tasks rather than temporary download files; default-folder and iOS downloads retain the existing engine. Adds destination access/space checks, pause/resume using HTTP Range, checkpoint persistence, cancellation cleanup, retry attempts, idle-sleep prevention, and library opening/completion notifications. The diff restores previously running downloads automatically on launch while leaving explicitly paused downloads paused, and uses destination-volume free space for diagnostics and low-space warnings. Caveat: Unmerged and closed by the contributor after review found major unresolved problems; not an established shipped contract. Reviewers reported progress stuck at 0% and incomplete cancellation cleanup, contradicting contributor test results. Later tests still reported micro-hangs and slow-drive pause-button delays; the planned 190 GB test was not documented as completed. The body describes paused-only restart restoration and lock/GCD-based internals, whereas the diff and later comments introduce automatic restoration and separate buffer/file-writer actors. Claimed ETag/Last-Modified validation is not wired up: the delegate accepts HTTP 200 even for resumed requests without validating ranges. Direct writes use the final .zim filename, and starting at offset zero deletes an existing destination instead of preserving the existing overwrite-warning workflow. Reviewers requested explicit handling for destination changes, unavailable drives, and existing/partial files; these remained unresolved. The claimed localization-file additions are absent from the supplied diff. Contributor compilation and testing reports do not establish runtime correctness. |

Also loads donation-button eligibility asynchronously, shortens directory-monitor polling from 500 ms to 50 ms, and removes the iPad opened-files programmatic detail-navigation workaround. Caveat: Marked merged; runtime correctness is not established. Despite the body's readiness claim, full Stripe modernization is explicitly deferred, with @preconcurrency imports and unchecked/unsafe concurrency annotations retained; a reviewer requests issues for remaining work. Removing the iPad navigation workaround has no demonstrated replacement for that specific selection-driven path in the supplied diff. Database and library-refresh tests/test doubles are deleted; parser tests are migrated to Swift Testing. The coverage comment reports a comparison behind main and fewer measured files/lines, not runtime verification. |

| [#1666 — Add support of cookies (traditional javascript API)](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/1666) **U** 2026-08-16 | persistence / D | CLOSED-UNMERGED: not an implemented fix. Unmerged proposal to emulate document.cookie for ZIM content: store values per ZIM UUID, persist dated cookies in Defaults, keep session cookies in memory, filter expired cookies on native reads, and clear stored cookies on unlink/delete. Caveat: Closed in favor of PR #1679. Contrary to the claimed browser-like behavior, the JS setter never updates the getter's local map, so writes are not immediately readable. Restoration occurs at navigation commit, not demonstrably before page scripts. Parsing prioritizes expires over max-age and ignores path/domain scope. Several review concerns are addressed in the supplied diff, including JSON serialization, preserving additional '=' characters, and use of the supplied time; this is not runtime verification. Submitted review: Reviews require cookie expiry support and iCloud backup, and leave eviction, JavaScript deletion, and overwrite behavior unclarified. | [#1679 — Support JS document.cookies](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-apple/pull/1679) **M** 2026-08-16 | persistence / D | Adds a document.cookie shim with immediate JS-side reads and writes, expiry/deletion parsing, and per-ZIM JSON persistence in Defaults. The diff establishes a local Documents file, not iCloud synchronization or cross-device conflict handling. No Defaults migration is provided; the author says the earlier storage had not reached production. Despite a 'Fixed' reply to the atomic-write review, first-file creation still uses createFile without atomic replacement. No shutdown flush for pending debounced writes is shown. Submitted review: Automated overview describes replacing Defaults persistence with debounced JSON-file writes in a location intended for iCloud sync/backup. Intended placement is not verification of actual iCloud sync, backup, or restore behavior; seven generated comments are not supplied. |

Caveat: Merged; the body reports macOS testing only, using a simplified test ZIM after difficulty with devdocs. A suppressed comment specifically warns that an unawaited cookieStore.delete(key) cleanup Promise can leave cookies behind and affect subsequent tests. |
